Q: What createds the change in light reflected off of a CD?
A: "The change in light reflected off of a CD is created by the alternating regions of heated and unaltered dye on a CD-R. When the laser hits a groove on a CD, it will not reflect any light, making it a \"1\". When the laser hits a bump or any other surface on the CD, a light reflection will appear, making it a \"0\". This modulation of reflected light is how the digital information is recovered from the CD."
